## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？

```

```

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼できる順番どおりの届け」、UDPは「速さ優先で届けっぱなし（届いた保証なし）」が一番の違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tus commits locales encima de los cambios más recientes de la rama principal y mantener un historial lineal, siempre que nadie más trabaje sobre esa rama. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, porque rebase reescribe commits y eso rompería el trabajo de otras personas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据完整有序地到达；UDP 是不可靠的无连接传输，只管快速发出去，不保证送达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared feature branch to keep history linear before it goes up. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have based work on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/user.test.ts
```

只写文件名片段也可以，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test user
```

如果想只跑文件里某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字筛选：

```bash
bun test test/user.test.ts -t "creates a user"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only record of every change a database intends to make, written to durable storage before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, the database replays the log on restart to finish or undo those changes, so committed work survives and partial work doesn't corrupt the data.
```

- 改动在这个 PR 的分支上提交，不合并、不推送、不转正式 PR（保持 draft）。
- 完成后简要说明：改了哪些锁、每处怎么改、测试结果。

### past-rust-haswell-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快
  [partner ack line] 好，接着 10 月 23 日那个「haswell 上 Rust 构建太慢」的活，把增量构建也提速，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task rust_haswell] 用户说：「我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快」。

已定的决定：沿用你之前的做法（sccache + mold，冷构建已从 14 分钟降到 6 分钟），在同一分支上继续。

要做的：
1. 在 haswell 上测量增量构建的现状（改动一个文件后重新构建的耗时），给出基线数字。
2. 找出增量构建慢的原因（例如 incremental 设置、codegen-units、链接器、依赖重编译、sccache 对增量的影响等），做能确认有效的改动。
3. 改完后再次测量增量构建耗时，和基线对比，并确认冷构建没有变差。

完成标准：报告里要有改动前后的增量构建耗时、改动内容，以及验证命令的输出。

不在范围内：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。改动只留在你的分支上提交。

地点：haswell 上的这个仓库（hawdb-rs），沿用原来的分支和工作区。
  [also used tools] task_find
```

  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁票价看板", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1880 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五京沪高铁，600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty" style="display:none">这次没有 600 元以下的班次</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var LIMIT = 600;
  function render(d) {
    d = d || {};
    var trains = (d.trains || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
    var cheap = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < LIMIT; });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = cheap.length + ' 班';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = (d.updated ? '更新于 ' + d.updated + '，' : '') + '第 ' + (d.day || 3) + ' 天';
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = '二等座' + (t.seats != null ? '余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '');
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.style.color = t.price < LIMIT ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
      val.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById('empty').style.display = cheap.length ? 'none' : 'block';
  }
  var got = ducter.get('board');
  Promise.resolve(got).then(render);
  ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('board')).then(render); });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"board":{"day":3,"updated":"11-23","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}}

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with Night 2 (181k writes/s, p99 4.0 ms) and Night 3 (182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms), and each new night's result will land on it.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1764 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">hawdb write benchmark, every night at 23:00</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Each night's result lands here.</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c];
    });
  }
  function render(runs) {
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
    if (!runs.length) {
      list.innerHTML = "";
      empty.style.display = "";
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = "none";
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    document.getElementById("latest").textContent =
      last.writes + " writes/s";
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent =
      last.night + " · p99 " + last.p99 + " · compaction stalls " + last.stalls;
    list.innerHTML = runs.slice().reverse().map(function (r) {
      return '<div class="k-row">' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(r.night) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + esc(r.p99) + ' · stalls ' + esc(r.stalls) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + esc(r.writes) + ' writes/s</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join("");
  }
  function load() {
    var v = ducter.get("runs");
    if (v && typeof v.then === "function") v.then(render);
    else render(v);
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 花销合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
</div>
<div id="days"></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>哪天</label><select class="k-select" id="dsel"></select></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="name" placeholder="如：午饭、地铁"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="newday">加一天</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const KEY = 'days';
let days = [];
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const money = n => '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '');
const sumOf = d => d.items.reduce((a, i) => a + i.amount, 0);
const total = () => days.reduce((a, d) => a + sumOf(d), 0);
const count = () => days.reduce((a, d) => a + d.items.length, 0);

function render() {
  const t = total(), n … pty">还没有哪一天。点下面「记一笔」，再点「加一天」开始记。</div>';
  const sel = $('dsel').value;
  $('dsel').innerHTML = days.map((d, i) => '<option value="' + i + '">第' + (i + 1) + '天</option>').join('');
  if (sel !== '' && days[+sel]) $('dsel').value = sel;
  ducter.summary(n ? n + ' 笔,合计 ' + money(t) : '还没记');
}

function save() { ducter.set(KEY, days); render(); }

function load(v) {
  if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = []; } }
  days = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}

$('days').onclick = e => {
  const r = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!r) return;
  days[+r.dataset.d].items.splice(+r.dataset.j, 1);
  save();
};

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const name = $('name').value.trim();
  const amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  const di = +$('dsel').value;
  if (!name || !(amt > 0) || !days[di]) return;
  days[di].items.push({ name: name, amount: amt });
  $('name').value = '';
  $('amt').value = '';
  save();
};

$('newday').onclick = () => {
  days.push({ items: [] });
  save();
  $('dsel').value = String(days.length - 1);
};

render();
Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const sum = xs.reduce((a, x) => a + Number(x.yuan || 0), 0);
  const totalEl = document.getElementById("total");
  const countEl = document.getElementById("count");
  const ul = document.getElementById("list");
  if (xs.length) {
    totalEl.textContent = "¥" + sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    totalEl.classList.remove("k-none");
    countEl.textContent = "共 " + xs.length + " 笔";
  } else {
    totalEl.textContent = "还没记";
    totalEl.classList.add("k-none");
    countEl.textContent = "记了之后这里显示合计";
  }
  ul.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const li = document.createElement("li");
    li.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("span");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    li.appendChild(main);
    li.appendChild(val);
    ul.appendChild(li);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? "3 笔,合计 ¥" + sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

    editId = id; $('t').value = f.time; $('ml').value = f.ml;
    $('form').open = true; $('del').hidden = false; $('cancel').hidden = false; $('add').textContent = '保存';
  }
  $('add').onclick = function () {
    var time = $('t').value.trim() || nowHM();
    var ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time) || !(ml > 0)) {
      $('msg').textContent = '时间如 06:10，奶量填毫升数'; $('msg').hidden = false; return;
    }
    if (editId) {
      feeds = feeds.map(function (f) { return f.id === editId ? { id: f.id, date: f.date, time: time, ml: ml } : f; });
    } else {
      feeds.push({ id: String(Date.now()), date: today(), time: time, ml: ml });
    }
    resetForm(); save();
  };
  $('del').onclick = function () {
    feeds = feeds.filter(function (f) { return f.id !== editId; });
    resetForm(); save();
  };
  $('cancel').onclick = resetForm;
  $('form').addEventListener('toggle', function () { if (!$('form').open && editId) resetForm(); });
  function load(v) { feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; render(); }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
  ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":"f1","date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":"f2","date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf as "PRs waiting on someone." Tick each PR as it lands and the count updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2200 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <span class="k-sum-label">Still open</span>
  <span class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</span>
  <span class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one as it lands</span>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<style>
.k-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer}
.k-row input{width:18px;height:18px;flex:0 0 auto;accent-color:var(--d-accent)}
.k-row-main{flex:1;color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px;text-align:right}
.landed .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<script>
var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
var landed = [];

function draw(){
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  PRS.forEach(function(p){
    var on = landed.indexOf(p.id) !== -1;
    var row = document.createElement('label');
    row.className = 'k-row' + (on ? ' landed' : '');
    var box = document.createElement('input');
    box.type = 'checkbox';
    box.checked = on;
    box.addEventListener('change', function(){
      if (box.checked) { if (landed.indexOf(p.id) === -1) landed.push(p.id); }
      else { landed = landed.filter(function(x){ return x !== p.id; }); }
      ducter.set('landed', landed);
      draw();
    });
    var main = document.createElement('span');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = p.repo + ' · ' + p.title;
    var sub = document.createElement('span');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = p.wait;
    row.appendChild(box);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var open = PRS.length - landed.length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = landed.length ? landed.length + ' landed' : 'Tick each one as it lands';
  ducter.summary(open + ' open, ' + landed.length + ' landed');
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
  landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  draw();
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
    landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    draw();
  });
});
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","wait":"Weizhen's review since Mon"},{"id":"m10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","wait":"CI failing"},{"id":"h1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Alice since Tue"},{"id":"d418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","wait":"Draft"}]}

```

  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2656 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有花销，点下面记下第一笔</div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label for="trip">出差名称</label><input id="trip" class="k-input" placeholder="比如 北京出差"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips"><button class="k-chip" data-cat="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-cat="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额</label><input id="amt" class="k-num" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function items(){return ducter.get("items")||[]}
function draw(){
  const xs=items();
  const list=$("list");list.innerHTML="";
  let total=0;
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const v=Number(x.amt)||0;total+=v;
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row"; … main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent="¥"+v.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  });
  $("empty").style.display=xs.length?"none":"block";
  if(xs.length){
    $("total").textContent="¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    $("sub").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  }else{
    $("total").innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    $("sub").textContent="记下花销后这里显示合计";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN"):"还没记");
}
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.cat;
  document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"));
});
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=items();
  const d=new Date();
  const day=d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"未命名出差",cat:cat,day:day,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","cat":"交通","day":"2026-11-20","note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","cat":"住宿","day":"2026-11-20","note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amt":1036}]}

```
